Survivors are collected flat (which is\n// what hasRealStockRow and the picking rollup need to see) and folded back into\n// the tree at the end, because that is the shape the document is stored in.\n// ==========================================================================\nconst childrenOf = (row) => (Array.isArray(row.children) ? row.children : []);\nconst isBundleParentRow = (row) =>\n  Boolean(row.item_bundle_id) && !row.material_id;\n\n// Survivors, by identity -- the loop mutates rows in place, so this only has to\n// record which ones made it through.\nconst survived = new Set();\n\nconst roundQty = (value) => Math.round((parseFloat(value) || 0) * 1000) / 1000;\n\n// A bundle is delivered as a whole: every item under it is the bundle's own\n// quantity times the number of bundles. { expected, actual } : null;\n};\n\n// Build flags map from Get Items (only populated when picking_mode=Auto ran).\n// Used to detect description-only rows: stock_control=0 AND show_delivery=0.\nconst itemDataRaw = {{node:search_node_w1NxF9TX.data.data}} || [];\nconst itemDataArray = Array.isArray(itemDataRaw) ?
